# Master Source Ledger

Step 1 output, consolidated on 2026-09-27 from the nine chunk ledgers in `_build/01-ledger/` (SP1–SP5 and BP1–BP4). It maps everything the two earlier programs teach onto the new 28-module blueprint and says, item by item, whether to skip it, deepen it, decide it fresh or leave it out. Every later step reads the compact guide in `_build/01-ledger-digest.md` first and comes here for detail by ID.

## How to read this ledger

- **Sources.** "SP" is the 10-part YouTube authority program (`_build/sources/s-plus-youtube-mastery.txt`). "BP" is the 20-module content and marketing program (`_build/sources/20-module-blueprint.md`). These names belong to the build only: modules never name, cite or point back to either program.
- **Rows.** Each row is one distinct item, with a stable ID (L-001 onward) grouped by theme. An item joins every chunk-ledger row that teaches the same thing, so an item taught in both programs sits in one row with both locations. Nothing was dropped: all 4,663 chunk rows are listed by ID and name in the last column of exactly one row. The chunk ledgers keep the full "What it says" text for each chunk row.
- **Tags, resolved across both programs.** SKIP = covered deeply somewhere, so it is assumed knowledge and never re-taught. DEEPEN = shallow everywhere; the direction says what the new depth is. RETHINK = the programs disagree, or the locked spec changes the premise; the question says what to decide fresh, on merit. OUT = excluded by the spec. Order of precedence: OUT, then RETHINK, then SKIP, then DEEPEN. Where a row settles a DEEPEN or RETHINK, its known, uncontested parts (for example a structure the new work extends) travel with it.
- **Positions.** Each RETHINK row ends with "Positions": what each program says, so the fresh decision can weigh both on merit. Neither wins by default.
- **Columns.** "Source tags" counts the chunk rows behind the item by program and chunk-ledger tag (S, D, R, O). "Where" gives source sections and line numbers (SP § = part.section; BP M = module); items spread over many sections show part or module spans instead. "Chunk rows covered" lists every chunk row folded into the item.
- **Gaps.** In-scope topics that neither program teaches have no source rows, so they are not rows here. They are listed with IDs G-01 onward in section e of the digest.
- **Renaming.** Items marked "Rename if reused" carry signature terms from the earlier programs. Any module that reuses the idea as a bridge coins a fresh name.
